# Innea starting population.
# Per-area totals come from map/area_population_targets.txt (hand-authored).
# Within each area they are split by:  dev^1.90 * (topography*vegetation*climate*rgo)^0.25 * jitter
#   jitter = seeded lognormal, sigma 0.18, seed 'innea-pops-v1' (deterministic per location)
# size is in THOUSANDS of people (vanilla Earth totals 393,896 = ~394 million).
#
# FIRST PASS: the whole population of a location is a single peasants pop.
# Splitting it into tribesmen / burghers / clergy / nobles / slaves comes later.

# Hand adjustments:
#   fuad has a floor of 1000k (not a cap)
#   zuwar + bayd + sire damped to 85% of their natural share, surplus to fuad
#   lower_falfedo_area flattened: weights ^ 0.85 before distributing the area target
#   central_teltran_area flattened: weights ^ 0.85 before distributing the area target
